mega_slots: 6
ai: Ace Pokemon
plan: First two useful turns: Zapdos attacks while Altaria sets Tailwind or uses Helping Hand only when it materially improves the exchange. Turn two brings Enamorus into a favorable physical/special matchup or continues pressure. Alternate deployment: Altaria supports Enamorus's Superpower/Contrary growth without making it a purely special attacker. Reserves & disruption: Talonflame is a Choice Band entry-pressure reserve, Noivern a special pivot, and Mega Skarmory a separate Iron Defense/Body Press endgame. Counterplay & audit: Rock/Ice pressure, Taunt, Wide Guard where relevant, special attacks into Skarmory and Haze. Disposition: Preserve the broad flying identity; benchmark Altaria's support contribution and avoid six interchangeable attacks.
crack: Rock/Ice pressure, Taunt, Wide Guard where relevant, special attacks into Skarmory and Haze. Disposition: Preserve the broad flying identity; benchmark Altaria's support contribution and avoid six interchangeable attacks.

## E0297 TRAINER_MYLES class=ace
strategy: REDIRECTION,SETUP
mega_slots: NONE
ai: Conservative
plan: First two useful turns: Togekiss redirects when it preserves a worthwhile Lucario attack; otherwise Thunder Wave or Air Slash may be better. Turn two chooses the attacker that best uses the protected slot. Alternate deployment: Togekiss supports Serperior's Leaf Storm growth or Whiscash's setup on a later safe entry. Reserves & disruption: Magmortar is immediate special spread, Miltank utility physical pressure and Whiscash a distinct Dragon Dance mode. Counterplay & audit: Spread damage, Taunt, powder-independent disruption and Fairy/Ground pressure. Disposition: Keep; never credit all back-line setup plans to one impossible simultaneous protected board.
crack: Spread damage, Taunt, powder-independent disruption and Fairy/Ground pressure. Disposition: Keep; never credit all back-line setup plans to one impossible simultaneous protected board.

## E0298 TRAINER_PAT class=ace
strategy: NONE
mega_slots: 4
ai: Conservative

No allied Electric hit is authored to guarantee Motor Drive. Counterplay & audit: Physical mitigation, priority, Grass/Ground/Fairy pressure and special bulk against the alternate mode. crack: Physical mitigation, priority, Grass/Ground/Fairy pressure and special bulk against the alternate mode. Disposition: Keep; don't invent a Motor Drive activation because two Electric-associated species are present.

## E0303 TRAINER_GABBY_AND_TY_6 class=regular
strategy: SETUP,TAILWIND
mega_slots: NONE
ai: Conservative
plan: First two useful turns: Exploud can lock into Boomburst beside Telepathy Noivern while Noivern supplies Tailwind or Super Fang. Turn two rechecks the ally, ability state and Choice lock before repeating spread sound. Alternate deployment: Telepathy Gardevoir is the second safe Boomburst partner and a special setup alternative. Reserves & disruption: Sylveon is not immune to Boomburst; use a fresh Hyper Voice lock, a legal switch or another safe line before it shares the field with Exploud. Counterplay & audit: Wide Guard, removing/suppressing the immunity partner, special mitigation and Choice-lock exploitation.
